# Source-to-active mapping

Source: PR #6, `2e2caa4b14b8af0464a836988ec1967336762b78`. The [index](../README.md) defines classifications and matrix coverage.

Each group retains distinct commands in the active matrix. `vN/pM/cK` maps source parameter/loop variant N, invocation M, to command K in that matrix row. Python variants enumerate decorators from bottom to top, with the bottom decorator varying slowest and each value list in source order. Rust variants count assertion calls in source loop order; the property-only overwide function counts its formatter calls. Rust `p2` uses the reviewed expected output as input. Equal inputs do not imply equal invocations.
